In online A/B tests, ClockRoPE demonstrates consistent improvements in valued engagement metrics, and has been successfully deployed in production-scale generative retrieval system at a major video-sharing platform.

</details>


### [105] [Q-Steer: Action-Value Guidance for Molecular Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26391)
*Xinyu Wang,Jinbo Bi,Minghu Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Q-Steer是一种用于分子语言模型的rollout-time动作价值引导原语，通过在采样logits中添加归一化的价值奖励，改善延迟反馈下的分子优化性能，不改变在线oracle预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子优化中的延迟反馈问题：oracle-limited设置中，只有在完整分子生成后才能获得奖励，而每个rollout需要许多局部下一个token决策。这种延迟反馈接口使得分子策略优化变得短视，优化器可能知道分子好但不知道哪些中间动作使其变好。

Method: 引入Q-Steer，使用离线训练且冻结的前缀-动作价值评分器(PAVS-Q)来估计在部分SMILES前缀下采取候选下一个token的下游奖励，然后将归一化的价值奖励添加到采样logits中。优化器更新规则和在线oracle预算保持不变。

Result: 在PMO23上使用固定的10,000次在线调用预算，对两个分子语言模型主干和四个优化器的完整因子研究表明，Q-Steer在所有八个主干-优化器单元中都提高了平均有效唯一分数，宏观平均分数增益在+0.033到+0.049之间，每个单元有18-20个任务获胜。机制控制显示动作身份很重要：前缀广播价值几乎中性，而打乱的动作价值会损害性能。

Conclusion: Q-Steer作为一个可重用的rollout-time动作价值包装器，在不改变在线oracle预算的情况下，提高了跨优化器家族和策略主干的平均分子优化奖励。

Abstract: Oracle-limited molecular optimization gives reward only after a complete molecule is generated, while each rollout requires many local next-token decisions. This delayed-feedback interface makes molecular policy optimization myopic: an optimizer can learn that a molecule was good without knowing which intermediate actions made it good. We introduce Q-Steer, a rollout-time action-value steering primitive for molecular language models. Q-Steer uses an offline-trained and frozen prefix-action value scorer, PAVS-Q, that estimates the downstream reward of taking a candidate next token under a partial SMILES prefix, then adds a normalized value bonus to sampling logits. The optimizer update rule and online oracle budget are unchanged; the claim is fixed-online-oracle performance, not equal total compute. On PMO23 with a fixed 10,000-call online budget, complete factorial studies across two molecular language-model backbones and four optimizers show that Q-Steer improves mean valid-unique score in all eight backbone-optimizer cells, with positive macro mean-score gains between +0.033 and +0.049 and 18-20 task wins per cell. Mechanism controls show that action identity matters: prefix-broadcast values are nearly neutral, while shuffled action values harm performance. These results support Q-Steer as a reusable rollout-time action-value wrapper that improves average molecular optimization reward across optimizer families and policy backbones without changing the online oracle budget.

</details>


### [106] [Flow Map Learning via Nongradient Vector Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26398)
*Mark Goldstein,Anshuk Uppal,Raghav Singhal,Aahlad Puli,Rajesh Ranganath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SGFlow是一种新的流映射学习方法，通过非保守动力学和驻点保证，绕过传统方法中的可逆性约束和昂贵迭代微分，在CIFAR图像基准上实现了有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散和流基模型虽然受益于简单的回归损失，但推理时需要积分导致显著开销。一致性模型通过直接学习ODE轨迹上的流映射来解决这个问题，但现有方法面临计算挑战，如需要模型逆或通过迭代模型调用的反向传播，并且不能总是证明期望的ODE流映射是损失的解。

Method: SGFlow通过跟随具有驻点在期望流映射的非保守动力学来训练模型，从而计算ODE解和隐含速度，绕过了显式可逆性约束和通过模型迭代的昂贵微分。

Result: 在CIFAR图像基准上，没有单一方法在所有步数上都获得最佳FID：SGFlow在10个采样步数上获得最佳FID，并在其他步数上与流匹配、Meanflow和拉格朗日映射匹配保持竞争力，同时是唯一具有基于stopgrad动力学的驻点保证的方法。

Conclusion: SGFlow提供了一种有效学习流映射的方法，通过非保守动力学和驻点保证，在保持竞争力的图像生成性能的同时，解决了现有方法中的计算挑战和理论保证问题。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-based models benefit from simple regression losses, but inference incurs significant overhead because sampling requires integration. Consistency models address this by directly learning the flow maps along the ODE trajectory, opening a design space between one-step and many-step approaches. However, existing methods face computational challenges such as requiring model inverses or backpropagation through iterated model calls, and do not always prove that the desired ODE flow map is a solution to the loss. We introduce SGFlow, an approach for learning flow maps that bypasses explicit invertibility constraints and expensive differentiation through model iteration. SGFlow trains a model to compute both the ODE solutions and the implied velocity from scratch by following non-conservative dynamics with a stationary point at the desired flow map. On the CIFAR image benchmark, no single method attains the best FID at every step count: SGFlow attains the best FID at 10 sampling steps and remains competitive with flow matching, Meanflow, and Lagrangian map matching at other step counts, while being the only one with a proven stationary-point guarantee for its stopgrad-based dynamics.

</details>


### [107] [Examining the Efficacy of Graph Neural Network Message-Passing in Regression Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26404)
*Keith G. Mills,Aedan J. DeFrates,Joong Ho Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了图神经网络在回归任务中的表现，发现深度卷积GNN（特别是GEN）在回归任务中比基于注意力的GNN更有效，而其他经典的理论启发式GNN仍保持竞争力和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前GNN的评估和基准测试主要基于分类任务，导致新的GNN层主要在分类场景下进行评估。然而，GNN同样能够执行标量回归预测，但这类问题在设计新GNN时经常被忽视。现有的最佳分类GNN被先验或现成地用于回归问题，这可能不是最优选择。

Method: 该研究在多种回归场景中评估GNN层的效能，包括排名排序、误差最小化和洞察提取。比较了不同类型的GNN，包括深度卷积GNN（特别是GEN）、基于注意力的GNN以及其他经典的理论启发式GNN。

Result: 结果显示，深度卷积GNN（特别是GEN）在回归任务中比基于注意力的GNN更有效。同时，其他经典的理论启发式GNN在这些任务中仍然具有竞争力和效率。

Conclusion: GNN在回归任务中的表现需要专门评估，不能简单依赖分类任务中表现最佳的模型。深度卷积GNN在回归任务中表现优异，而经典的理论启发式GNN在效率和性能之间提供了良好的平衡。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNN) facilitate effective prediction on graph data such as molecules, media networks and neural network blueprints. GNNs facilitate prediction through message passing techniques which define how information flows from a node to its neighbors. Due to the ubiquity of the graph data type, the development of newer and better GNNs has garnered much interest in the machine learning community.
  However, GNN evaluation and benchmarking is primarily driven by classification tasks. Thus, prospective GNN message passing layers are evaluated on their ability to outperform prior work in classification contexts. In contrast, GNNs are equally capable of performing scalar regression prediction, yet this class of problem is often overlooked when proposing new GNNs while the best classification GNNs are utilized in an a priori or off-the-shelf manner for regression problems. In response, this paper studies the efficacy of GNN layers in a slew of regression contexts from rank ordering, error minimization and insight extraction. Results show that deep convolutional GNNs, particularly GEN, are more effective at these tasks than attention-based GNNs, while other classical, theoretically-inspired GNNs remain competitive and efficient.

</details>


### [108] [SCOUT: Per-Context Reset Curricula for Sparse-Reward Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26417)
*Siddharth Aphale,Ayushman Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SCOUT，一种针对稀疏奖励强化学习的自适应重置控制器，为每个任务上下文提供个性化课程，解决了全局统一调度在任务实例学习速度不同时可能失败的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏奖励强化学习中，从初始状态开始的rollout很少能到达任务后期阶段，传统重置课程使用统一的辅助移除计划可能失败，特别是当不同任务实例（上下文）学习速度不同时需要冲突的辅助练习量。

Method: SCOUT是一种在线、与学习器无关的重置控制器，仅使用二元rollout成功信号：持续成功时移除辅助，失败时恢复辅助，进度停滞时谨慎测试更难起点，不改变奖励、优化器或学习器本身。

Result: 在六个导航和操作任务中，脚手架访问提高了学习效率，在三个任务中实现了无辅助训练无法达到的成功。在构建的调度冲突中，所有测试的全局调度都至少有一个组无法解决，而SCOUT解决了所有组。SCOUT无需组标签，在组内和组间学习差异情况下都表现稳定。

Conclusion: 重置课程应在学习进度不同的尺度上移除辅助，SCOUT通过为每个上下文提供个性化课程实现了这一目标，相比全局统一调度更鲁棒，且不依赖组标签信息。

Abstract: Sparse-reward reinforcement learning often fails because rollouts from the unassisted evaluation start rarely reach later task stages. Reset curricula address this by starting some training rollouts from easier intermediate states, called scaffolds. Such a curriculum faces two decisions: scaffold access, obtaining informative starts, and scaffold allocation, deciding how quickly that assistance is removed. Most prior curricula pace removal on one shared schedule, which can fail when task instances, or contexts, learn at different rates. We introduce SCOUT, an online, learner-agnostic reset controller that gives every context its own curriculum. Using only binary rollout success, SCOUT removes assistance after sustained success, restores it after failure, and cautiously tests a harder start when progress stalls, without changing the reward, optimizer, or learner. A counting construction shows that synchronized global pacing can be insufficient when contexts need conflicting amounts of assisted practice. Across six navigation and manipulation settings, scaffold access improves learning and enables success in three where unassisted training fails within the reported budget. In a constructed pacing conflict, each tested global schedule leaves one group unsolved, while SCOUT solves both. Average success can conceal this failure, so we also report the least successful group. Group-level pacing works when learning differences follow known groups but can fail when they occur within one group. SCOUT needs no group labels and remains consistently strong in both cases. A reset curriculum should remove assistance at the scale where learning progress differs.

</details>


### [109] [Existence-Field Diffusion Model for Spatial Point Processes with Variable Cardinality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26428)
*Xiaoyin Pan,Christian R. Shelton,Rakshith Mahishi,Chengkuan Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于空间点过程建模的存在场扩散模型（EFDM），通过为每个潜在点关联存在变量来统一建模空间位置和基数，避免了离散的维度转换操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在建模空间点过程时，要么将基数建模与空间结构解耦，要么依赖离散的跨维度操作来修改点数，导致生成动态不灵活且不对称。需要一种能够统一建模空间位置和基数的灵活框架。

Method: 提出存在场扩散模型（EFDM），为每个潜在点关联一个存在变量来表示其存在程度。通过统一的扩散过程联合建模空间位置和基数，无需显式的离散转换操作。

Result: 该方法在具有变化基数的数据集上表现出改进的建模能力，为空间点过程的生成建模提供了灵活且通用的框架。

Conclusion: EFDM通过引入存在变量实现了空间点过程基数和空间配置的联合建模，避免了现有方法的局限性，为可变基数的空间点过程建模提供了更灵活的解决方案。

Abstract: We study generative modeling of spatial point processes (SPP), where both the number of points and their spatial configuration are governed by a joint distribution. While diffusion models have achieved strong performance in modeling complex distributions, extending them to variable-cardinality SPP remains challenging. Existing approaches either decouple the modeling of cardinality and spatial structure, or rely on discrete trans-dimensional operations to modify the number of points, resulting in inflexible and asymmetric generative dynamics. We propose the existence-field diffusion model (EFDM) for spatial point processes modeling, where each potential point is associated with an existence variable representing its degree of presence. This enables a unified diffusion process that jointly models both spatial locations and cardinality without requiring explicit discrete transitions. We demonstrate that our approach provides a flexible and general framework for generative modeling of spatial point processes, achieving improved modeling capability on datasets with varying cardinality.

</details>


### [110] [DHRCL:Training Code LLMs with Dense Hierarchical Rewards and Curriculum Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26457)
*Shuhang Wang,Ziming Li,Hui Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DHRCL是一个用于代码生成LLMs的强化学习框架，采用密集分层奖励和课程学习，通过语法验证、执行成功、单元测试通过率和AST结构相似性四个阶段渐进优化代码生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常依赖稀疏的结果奖励或静态组合异构的密集信号，但语法有效性、可执行性、功能正确性和结构组织描述了不同且渐进依赖的编程能力，需要更系统的训练方法。

Method: DHRCL将反馈分解为语法验证、执行成功、单元测试通过率和基于AST的结构相似性，并通过语法、执行、通过和结构三阶段课程组织这些信号。采用基于最近验证趋势的自动阶段持续时间确定，以及阶段感知的基于概率的令牌信用重新分配机制。

Result: 在统一的Qwen3-8B和KodCode协议下，DHRCL相比二进制、通过率、奖励模型和可验证密集奖励基线表现更优。在不同规模模型（Qwen3-4B、8B、14B）上，其优势随模型容量增加保持一致。

Conclusion: DHRCL通过分层奖励和课程学习有效解决了代码生成LLMs的强化学习问题，其阶段感知的信用分配机制和自动课程调度为代码生成能力提供了系统化的训练框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is a natural post-training paradigm for code-oriented large language models because generated programs can be evaluated through parsing, execution, unit tests, and structural analysis.However, existing methods often rely on sparse outcome rewards or statically combine heterogeneous dense signals, even though syntax validity, executability, functional correctness, and structural organization describe different and progressively dependent programming capabilities. We propose DHRCL, a reinforcement learning framework with Dense Hierarchical Rewards and Curriculum Learning. DHRCL decomposes feedback into syntax validation, execution success, unit-test pass rate, and AST-based structural similarity, and organizes these signals through a three-stage Syntax, Execution, Pass & Structural curriculum. Stage duration is determined automatically from recent validation trends rather than manually specified capability thresholds. We further introduce stage-aware probability-based token credit redistribution. The mechanism follows a consolidation-to-refinement principle: it emphasizes established token patterns during syntax-oriented optimization, applies uniform propagation for non-local execution feedback, and allocates more credit or blame to less-established token decisions during final functional optimization. Under a unified Qwen3-8B and KodCode protocol, the experiments compare DHRCL with binary, pass-rate, reward-model-based, and verifiable dense-reward baselines. We further evaluate DHRCL across Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, and Qwen3-14B backbones, showing that its advantage remains consistent as model capacity increases.

</details>


### [111] [Neural Architecture Search for Traffic Prediction: A Survey of Methods, Challenges, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26467)
*Truong Giang Vu,Li Yang,Richard W. Pazzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文综述了将神经架构搜索（NAS）应用于交通预测的现状，系统分析了不同NAS方法在交通时空数据上的应用，并讨论了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前交通预测中使用的深度学习模型（如图卷积网络、循环网络、Transformer）需要手动设计架构，这需要大量专家经验，且模型在不同城市和数据集上泛化能力差。神经架构搜索（NAS）提供了一种系统化的替代方案，可以自动搜索适合交通时空数据结构的模型架构。

Method: 按照搜索策略对NAS方法进行分类综述：基于梯度的方法、进化方法和一次性权重共享方法。分析每种方法如何设计搜索空间以覆盖交通的时空算子，以及搜索策略如何在成本和架构质量之间取得平衡。

Result: 系统梳理了NAS在交通预测中的应用现状，分析了不同方法的优缺点，并识别了当前研究的主要进展和局限性。

Conclusion: 提出了该领域的开放挑战：计算可扩展性（大型路网）、手动搜索空间设计、跨城市泛化、动态图结构，以及NAS在时空基础模型中的应用问题。指出了未来研究的方向。

Abstract: Traffic prediction is a core task in intelligent transportation systems, supporting applications such as adaptive signal control, route guidance, and ride-hailing dispatch. Deep learning models, including graph convolutional networks, recurrent networks, and Transformers, achieve strong results on standard benchmarks, but their architectures are designed by hand, requiring significant expert effort and producing models that often generalize poorly across cities and datasets. Neural Architecture Search (NAS) offers a systematic alternative to manual design. It automates the search over candidate architectures of deep learning models, finding designs that match the spatial-temporal structure of traffic data without manual trial and error. This survey reviews NAS methods applied to traffic prediction, organized by search strategy: gradient-based methods, evolutionary methods, and one-shot weight-sharing methods. For each category, we analyze how the search space is designed to cover spatial and temporal traffic operators, and how the search strategy balances cost against architecture quality. We also discuss open challenges, computational scalability to large road networks, manual search space design, cross-city generalization, dynamic graph structure, and the open question of NAS for spatial-temporal foundation models, and identify directions for future research.

</details>


### [112] [Learning Dynamic User Personas from Implicit Interaction Streams via Iterative Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26473)
*Haifeng Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IRIS框架通过从日常对话中提取行为信号，无需显式反馈即可从隐式交互流中学习动态用户画像，为个性化LLMs提供了可扩展的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化方法依赖显式偏好监督（如成对比较或人口统计属性），限制了在自然交互场景中的应用。需要直接从隐式交互流中学习用户画像的方法。

Method: IRIS框架从隐式交互流中提取行为信号，通过预测驱动的闭环迭代优化画像表示，无需显式反馈。评估基于行为预测、画像稳定性和决策预测。

Result: 在合成数据上，IRIS产生稳定画像并能区分不同用户。在真实Reddit AITA数据上，IRIS在100位作者中达到61.0%的决策预测准确率，优于静态画像、仅记忆检索和无个性化基线。

Conclusion: 隐式行为建模为个性化LLMs提供了可扩展的替代方案，为需要持续演化用户模型的自适应对话系统和具身智能体提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Personalizing large language models (LLMs) to individual users is essential for improving user experience, yet existing approaches typically rely on explicit preference supervision such as pairwise comparisons or demographic attributes, limiting their applicability in natural interaction settings. We propose IRIS, a framework that learns dynamic user personas directly from implicit interaction streams by extracting behavioral signals from everyday conversations and iteratively refining persona representations through a prediction-driven closed loop without requiring explicit feedback. We introduce an evaluation protocol based on behavior prediction, persona stability, and decision prediction. A proof-of-concept study on a synthetic interaction stream derived from public-domain autobiographical text shows that IRIS produces stable personas and distinguishes individual users while revealing limitations of memory-only approaches on recall-oriented metrics. We then validate IRIS on anonymized real-world Reddit r/AmItheAsshole (AITA) data, with personas built solely from each author's historical interactions. Across 100 authors, IRIS achieves the highest decision prediction accuracy among all evaluated methods (61.0%), outperforming static personas, memory-only retrieval, and no-personalization baselines. These results suggest that implicit behavioral modeling provides a scalable alternative to explicit preference learning for personalized LLMs and offers a practical foundation for adaptive conversational systems and embodied agents that require continuously evolving models of their users.

</details>


### [113] [Temporally Centered SIGReg Improves Multi-Task LeWorldModel Learning: From Analysis to Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26924)
*Chang Liu,Fei Suo,Yanzhou Jin,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Yaonan Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SIGReg正则化在单任务世界模型学习中有效，但在多任务训练中会导致表示混叠和性能下降。通过将SIGReg应用于时间中心残差而非潜在边际分布，解决了这一问题，显著提升了多任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SIGReg在单任务世界模型学习中能防止表示坍塌，但在多任务训练中效果不佳，导致下游行为克隆性能显著下降。研究发现边际高斯化会压缩任务相关潜在簇之间的分离度，引入表示混叠问题。

Method: 将SIGReg应用于时间中心残差而非潜在边际分布。这种方法不直接对簇中心分离度施加正则化压力，不要求整个潜在空间遵循单一各向同性高斯分布，同时保留了SIGReg的抗坍塌效果。

Result: 在LIBERO基准测试中，该方法将长期任务套件的下游成功率提高了1.7倍，四个套件的平均成功率从53.2%提升至73.6%。无需外部预训练，性能略优于从头训练的Diffusion Policy，接近大规模预训练策略基线的水平。

Conclusion: 边际高斯先验与多任务潜在结构存在结构性不兼容，通过将正则化应用于时间中心残差，提供了一条实现稳定且可扩展的端到端多任务世界模型学习的简单途径。

Abstract: Recent work on LeWorldModel (LeWM) has shown that the Sketched Isotropic Gaussian Regularizer (SIGReg) enables stable end-to-end world-model learning from pixels by regularizing the latent marginal distribution toward an isotropic Gaussian, thereby preventing representation collapse. While effective and elegant in single-task settings, this recipe does not extend reliably to multi-task training, leading to substantially worse downstream behavior-cloning performance. In this paper, we show that marginal Gaussianization compresses the separation between task-dependent latent clusters relative to within-cluster variation. This compression introduces representation aliasing across tasks and states, and makes the learned representations highly sensitive to small visual perturbations. To address this problem, we apply SIGReg to temporally centered residuals rather than to the latent marginal distribution. This surrogate target places no direct regularization pressure on the separation among cluster centers, removes the requirement that the full latent follow a single isotropic Gaussian, and retains the anti-collapse effect of SIGReg. On the LIBERO benchmark, our method improves downstream success on the long-horizon suite by 1.7x and raises the average success rate across four suites from 53.2% to 73.6%. Without external pretraining, it slightly outperforms Diffusion Policy trained from scratch and approaches the performance of large-scale pretrained policy baselines. These results reveal a structural incompatibility between marginal Gaussian priors and multi-task latent structure, and provide a simple route toward stable and scalable end-to-end multi-task world-model learning.

</details>


### [114] [Conformal Changepoint Localization and Root Cause Analysis with Corrupted Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26481)
*Seunghun Yu,Meiyi Zhu,Petar Popovski,Joonhyuk Kang,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出加权CONCH和加权CROC方法，通过降低可能被污染观测的权重来减小置信集大小，同时保持统计可靠性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工程系统监控中，需要在数据可能被污染（如异常值、传感器故障）的情况下，提供具有统计可靠性保证的变更点检测和根本原因分析，同时保持置信集的信息性。

Method: 提出加权CONCH（W-CONCH）和加权CROC（W-CROC），基于Huber型污染模型，利用二阶分类器不确定性信号对观测进行加权，并引入优化置信集大小的元学习过程。

Result: 实验表明，基于不确定性的加权方法在保持目标覆盖概率的同时，显著减小了置信集大小，适用于图像基准和真实世界数据集。

Conclusion: W-CONCH和W-CROC在数据可能被污染的情况下，通过智能加权机制有效平衡了统计可靠性和置信集信息量，为实际工程监控提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Detecting when the statistical behavior of an engineered system changes, and identifying which component is responsible, are core problems in the monitoring of telecommunication networks, robotic platforms, security infrastructure, and multi-agent systems. In safety- and mission-critical deployments, such decisions must be accompanied by statistical reliability guarantees rather than by point estimates alone. Conformal changepoint localization (CONCH) and conformal root cause analysis (CROC) meet this need by returning confidence sets that contain the true changepoint, or the true root-cause stream, with a user-specified probability, without parametric assumptions on the data-generating process. In practice, however, observations are frequently corrupted, e.g., by outliers, sensor faults, or adversarial perturbations. While the finite-sample coverage of these procedures is preserved under contamination, the resulting confidence sets can become uninformatively large. Adopting a Huber-type contamination model, this paper proposes weighted CONCH (W-CONCH) and weighted CROC (W-CROC), which downweight observations that are likely to be corrupted with the goal of reducing confidence set size when data may be corrupted. The weighting mechanism, derived from a formal bound on the unknown corrupted data densities, leverages pre-existing second-order classifier-based uncertainty signals, such as those produced by evidential deep learning or Bayesian learning. W-CONCH and W-CROC are further generalized by introducing a meta-learning procedure for the weights that optimizes a differentiable surrogate of the confidence set size. Experiments on image-based and real-world changepoint and root-cause benchmarks show that uncertainty-based weighting substantially reduces confidence set size while maintaining the target coverage.

</details>


### [115] [What Can Latent World Models Know? Physical Parameter Identifiability in Multimodal Predictive Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27017)
*Kaizhen Tan,Xin Xu,Siru Tao,Hanzhe Hong,Yang Feng,Heqing Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了潜在世界模型中物理参数的识别问题，发现在POKEWORLD和RH20T环境中，输入限制了可获取的知识，而预测目标决定了哪些物理参数被保留在潜在表示中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究潜在世界模型在预测未来时，其表示中实际包含了哪些物理量，以及决定这些物理量被包含的因素是什么。通过控制实验探究哪些物理参数能被模型学习到。

Method: 使用POKEWORLD交互环境（视觉相同但隐藏质量、阻力和接触刚度的物体），采用证书门控协议：首先验证每个参数是否可从原始观察中恢复，然后测量是否进入潜在表示。在RH20T环境中进行输入-目标因子分析，跨越两个机器人和4,258个episode。

Result: 识别性地图显示两个组织机制和一个前沿：输入限制可知内容，预测目标决定保留内容。刚度仅在预测触摸时进入潜在表示（R²=0.50 vs -0.02）。阻力是前沿，可恢复性证书0.89但预测目标下仅0.13，监督头可达0.45。RH20T实验证实了这些机制，只有完整多模态目标能预测超出持久性基线的力。

Conclusion: 目标结构决定了潜在表示获取哪些物理参数，额外数据仅改进已获取的参数。读取缓慢且基于比例类型的参数超出了这些目标获取的范围。

Abstract: A central premise of latent world models is that predicting the future forces a representation to internalize the physics of its environment. Which physical quantities does a trained latent actually contain, and what decides this? We answer with controlled interventions in POKEWORLD, an interactive environment whose visually identical objects hide mass, drag, and contact stiffness. A certificate-gated protocol first certifies each parameter as recoverable from raw observations, then measures whether it enters the latent, so a null result can be attributed to the objective rather than to the environment. The resulting identifiability map has two organizing mechanisms and one frontier. Inputs limit what can be known, while prediction targets decide what is retained. Stiffness enters the latent only when touch is forecast ($R^2=0.50$, compared with $-0.02$ when the same signal is merely fused into the input), and under single-step prediction a vision-only latent discards even perfectly visible object state. Drag marks the frontier. It carries a recoverability certificate of 0.89 yet plateaus near 0.13 under every deterministic prediction objective we test, while a supervised head on the same trunk reaches 0.45. Parameters whose readout is slow and ratio-type under the sensed coordinates fall outside what these objectives acquire. On RH20T, an input-target factorial across scaling curves reproduces both mechanisms across two robots and 4,258 episodes. Every arm missing information or prediction pressure stays flat over a fivefold data range, and only the full multimodal objective forecasts force beyond a persistence baseline, with held-out gains that grow with scale. Objective structure determines which physical parameters a latent acquires, and additional data improves only the parameters it already acquires.

</details>


### [116] [From Conceptual Hydrologic Models to Conceptually Interpretable Neural Networks: A Snow-Water Mass-Conserving-Perceptron Framework for Discovering Catchment-Scale Precipitation-Storage-Runoff Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26492)
*Yuan-Heng Wang,Hoshin V. Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将概念性水文模型重构为物理约束、概念可解释的神经网络框架，通过质量守恒感知机（MCP）单元在513个流域进行验证，发现2-5状态网络性能提升但边际效益递减，MCP网络参数更少且与LSTM性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立一种新的建模范式，将传统概念性水文模型转化为物理约束、概念可解释的神经网络，以结合水文模型的物理可解释性和神经网络的数据驱动优势，实现更优的流域水文表征。

Method: 开发基于质量守恒感知机（MCP）的雪水网络框架，将耦合的SOIL-MCP和SNOW-MCP两状态概念模型重构为质量守恒神经网络，评估不同状态数量（1-5状态）的单层网络性能，使用AIC和KGE指标选择平衡预测精度与复杂度的最优图表示。

Result: 在CONUS地区，中位KGEss从1状态网络的0.82提升到2状态网络的0.89和5状态网络的0.90，但2状态以上增益边际递减；MCP与LSTM选择达到相同中位KGEss 0.90，但MCP平均使用更少参数；AIC和KGE联合选择能识别平衡精度与复杂度的紧凑图表示。

Conclusion: MCP框架成功将概念性水文模型转化为物理约束神经网络，为确定水文表征所需的状态数量、类型和相互作用提供了经验基础，未来应测试对多种水文响应（径流、雪水当量、地下水储量）的联合训练。

Abstract: The Mass-Conserving Perceptron (MCP) establishes a modeling paradigm in which conceptual hydrologic models can be reformulated as physically constrained, conceptually interpretable neural networks. Here, we develop a snow-water MCP network framework and evaluate it across 513 CAMELS-US basins. We first recast a coupled two-state SOIL-MCP and SNOWMCP conceptual model as a mass-conserving neural network and show that the hydrologic-model and neural-network formulations achieve comparable predictive performance. We then examine cross-node state-information sharing within two-state HYDROMCP architectures and evaluate broader single-layer networks constructed from three types of interpretable MCP units with one to five states. Across CONUS, the median KGEss increases from 0.82 for one-state networks to 0.89 for two-state networks and 0.90 for five-state networks, suggesting diminishing aggregate gains beyond two states. Basin-specific MCP and LSTM selection yields the same median KGEss of 0.90, while the selected MCP networks use fewer parameters on average. Complementary AIC- and KGE-based selection identifies compact, basin-specific directed-graph representations that balance predictive accuracy and model complexity. These analyses provide an empirical basis for identifying the numbers, types, and interactions of states needed for hydrologic representation. Future studies should test joint training against multiple hydrologic responses, such as streamflow, snow water equivalent, and groundwater storage.

</details>


### [117] [From Interface to Inference: Eliciting Any-Order Inference from Any-Order Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26504)
*Seunggeun Kim,Jaeyeon Kim,Taekyun Lee,Yuyuan Chen,Yilun Du,Sham Kakade,Sitan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出两种方法来解决掩码扩散模型在任意顺序推理中的接口-推理差距，通过插入式掩码扩散和潜在空间掩码扩散来改善位置不确定性，并在Python代码生成和GSM8K数学推理任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多离散推理任务（如代码生成）本质上是非因果的，需要任意顺序推理。自回归语言模型缺乏原生任意顺序接口，而掩码扩散模型虽有任意顺序训练目标，但其接口并不自动支持任意顺序推理。这种接口-推理差距源于位置不确定性：固定画布的token级模型知道应该出现什么语义组件，但不知道放在哪里。

Method: 提出两种互补方法：1) 基于插入的掩码扩散（基于FlexMDM），通过插入操作放松固定位置承诺，支持跨非连续区域的生成；2) 潜在空间掩码扩散，将预测转移到更粗粒度的语义片段，支持潜在生成顺序的搜索。在Python代码生成任务上训练7B参数的FlexMDM，在GSM8K数学推理任务上训练125M参数的LatentMDM。

Result: 两种方法都诱导了不同的任意顺序推理行为，并提高了下游任务性能。插入式方法支持跨非连续区域的生成，潜在空间方法支持在更粗粒度语义片段上的搜索，两者都有效解决了位置不确定性问题。

Conclusion: 掩码扩散模型可以通过插入式和潜在空间方法克服接口-推理差距，实现真正的任意顺序推理。这两种互补方法为解决位置不确定性问题提供了有效途径，并在代码生成和数学推理任务上展示了实际效果。

Abstract: Many discrete reasoning tasks, such as code generation, are inherently non-causal: programmers move between high-level structure and local details, a process we call any-order inference. For autoregressive language models, which lack a native any-order interface, non-causal abilities such as infilling and next-edit prediction require hand-designed mechanisms. Can we instead design models that natively support any-order inference? Masked diffusion models have recently emerged as compelling candidates, as their any-order training objective naturally offers an any-order prediction interface. This interface, however, does not automatically yield any-order inference. We demonstrate that this interface-inference gap stems from positional uncertainty: fixed-canvas, token-level models may know what semantic component should appear without knowing where to place it. In light of this, we propose two complementary approaches: (1) Insertion-based masked diffusion, building on FlexMDM (Kim et al, 2025), relaxes fixed-position commitments via insertions, enabling generation across non-contiguous regions. (2) Latent-space masked diffusion shifts prediction to coarser semantic segments, enabling search over latent generation orders. Empirically, we train a 7B FlexMDM for Python coding and a 125M LatentMDM for GSM8K and show that both approaches induce distinct any-order inference behaviors and improve downstream performance. We release our codebase at https://github.com/SeunggeunKimkr/genuine-any-order.

</details>


### [118] [Collaborative Weighting with Pessimistic Critic for Mitigating Overestimation in Off-Policy Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26509)
*Gong Gao,Xiao Lai,Ziqi Xie,Guojie Chen,Xianhui Liu,Weidong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CWAC 是一个用于连续控制的深度强化学习框架，通过分布评论家和协作加权机制解决TD学习中的噪声目标和过估计偏差问题，提高训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统连续控制中的离策略强化学习算法依赖神经价值函数近似，但TD学习引入噪声目标导致非平稳优化，贪婪策略更新会放大早期估计误差，递归传播这些误差导致过估计偏差和训练稳定性下降。现有方法通过优先采样或修改价值学习目标来缓解，但往往过度强调由有限数据覆盖或引导误差引起的高不确定性转移，进一步放大偏差。

Method: 提出协作加权演员-评论家 (CWAC) 框架，采用分布评论家建模回报不确定性，引入协作加权机制联合重新加权TD误差和不确定性，从可靠样本中稳健学习同时抑制噪声更新。此外，通过从回报分布中采样的随机悲观价值估计方案，有效缓解策略改进期间的误差传播。CWAC可无缝集成到SAC、TD3和DDPG等现有离策略算法框架中。

Result: 经验结果表明，该方法在多种模拟任务中显著提升了性能表现。

Conclusion: CWAC通过显式考虑价值估计中的预测不确定性，提供了一种统一的解决方案，能够有效缓解误差传播，提高连续控制任务中强化学习的稳定性和性能。

Abstract: Deep off-policy reinforcement learning algorithms for continuous control typically rely on neural value function approximation to guide policy improvement. However, temporal-difference (TD) learning introduces noisy targets, resulting in non-stationary optimization, while greedy policy updates amplify early-stage estimation errors. The recursive propagation of such errors leads to persistent overestimation bias and degraded training stability in actor-critic methods. Existing approaches attempt to alleviate this issue via prioritized sampling or modified value learning objectives, but often overemphasize high-uncertainty transitions caused by limited data coverage or bootstrapping errors, thereby further amplifying bias.In this paper, we propose Collaborative Weighting Actor-Critic (CWAC), a unified framework that explicitly accounts for predictive uncertainty in value estimation. CWAC employs distributional critic to model return uncertainty and introduces a collaborative weighting mechanism that jointly reweights TD-errors and uncertainty, enabling robust learning from reliable samples while suppressing noisy updates. In addition, we incorporate a stochastic pessimistic value estimation scheme via sampling from the return distribution, which effectively mitigates error propagation during policy improvement. CWAC can be seamlessly integrated into existing off-policy algorithm frameworks such as SAC, TD3, and DDPG with minimal overhead. Empirical results demonstrate that our proposed method significantly enhances performance across a diverse range of simulated tasks. Our code is publicly available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CWAC-348E.

</details>


### [119] [HiFloat4 Format for End-To-End Reinforcement Learning Post-Training of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26515)
*Hei Yi Mak,Shadan Golestan,Hoang Le,Mehran Taghian Jazi,Yunke Peng,Yaoyuan Wang,Yao Wang,Junsong Wang,Tianchi Hu,Fengchen He,Guipeng Hu,Tanzila Rahman,Anandharaju Durai Raju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次提出端到端FP4 RL训练后量化方法，通过Rollout-ResQ技术解决激活值量化导致的性能下降问题，使用HiF4格式在4位精度下接近全精度性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RL训练后量化面临性能下降问题，研究发现主要原因是rollout激活量化中的异常值导致大量激活值在FP4精度下下溢为零，而非训练侧量化误差

Method: 提出Rollout Residual Quantization (Rollout-ResQ)：在FP4 rollout矩阵乘法中添加单残差校正项，采用硬件友好的稀疏模式，轻量级地恢复异常值驱动的下溢导致的精度损失；配合HiFloat4 (HiF4)格式，通过三级分层缩放在4位预算下保持分辨率

Result: 在Qwen2.5-3B和Qwen2.5-Math-7B上，Rollout-ResQ配合HiF4格式将BF16精度差距从4.9%缩小到1.1%；在MXFP4标准上，将差距从13.6%缩小到5.3%，表明FP4格式选择是决定可恢复精度上限的关键因素

Conclusion: HiF4格式是实现端到端FP4 RL训练后量化的关键格式，Rollout-ResQ是使BF16精度差距可缩小的激活侧机制，使完全量化FP4 RL接近全精度性能成为可能

Abstract: We present, to our knowledge, the first end-to-end FP4 RL post-training, in which both the rollout and training policies, including their forward and backward passes, operate at 4-bit precision. A systematic study reveals that the dominant source of degradation in FP4 RL is not training-side quantization error but rollout activation quantization: outliers stretch the dynamic range so far that a large number of activation values underflow to zero under FP4. Counterintuitively, restoring the training policy to higher precision while keeping the rollout in FP4 makes accuracy worse than full FP4 baseline, exposing rollout-training mismatch as the principal failure mode and ruling out standard pretraining-style fixes. We address this with Rollout Residual Quantization (Rollout-ResQ): a single residual correction term constrained to a hardware-friendly sparsity pattern, added only to the FP4 rollout matmul -- a lightweight correction that recovers most of the precision lost to outlier-driven underflow without inflating the rollout's compute footprint. On Qwen2.5-3B and Qwen2.5-Math-7B, Rollout-ResQ paired with the HiFloat4 (HiF4) format -- whose three-level hierarchical scaling preserves resolution under FP4's tight 4-bit budget -- closes the accuracy gap to BF16 from 4.9% to 1.1%, bringing fully quantized FP4 RL within striking distance of full precision. Applied to the open-standard MXFP4, the same recipe narrows the gap from 13.6% to 5.3%, revealing that FP4 format choice is a key factor that determines the ceiling on recoverable accuracy. Together, these results establish HiF4 as the enabling format for end-to-end FP4 RL post-training, and Rollout-ResQ as the activation-side mechanism that makes the gap to BF16 closable.

</details>


### [120] [From Unsupervised Subgroups to Hypothetical State-Intervention Policies: An Evaluation of Selected Subgrouping Methods in Observational Health Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26521)
*Vasundhara Acharya,Bulent Yener*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于预处理特征构建子组的新框架，用于预算约束下的政策优先排序，避免传统子组分析的不稳定性问题。在糖尿病和吸烟数据集上测试了多种聚类方法，发现不同方法产生的政策效用相似但优先排序个体可能不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统子组分析在观察性生物医学数据中存在问题：每个个体只观察一种暴露状态，无法获得真实个体治疗效果，因果结构不确定，导致结论不稳定且难以解释。研究探索是否可以不使用暴露、结果或治疗效果估计信息，仅基于预处理特征构建子组，作为预算约束政策优先排序的可解释单元。

Method: 提出结合因果发现指导的协变量选择、发现-评估样本分割、归纳式无监督聚类、不确定性感知子组选择以及保留样本的双重稳健政策评估框架。比较了K-means、硬性/加权/随机Fuzzy C-means、贝叶斯高斯混合模型，以及监督式因果森林衍生的CATE树作为对照方法。

Result: 在PIMA Indians糖尿病数据集（肥胖转非肥胖、高血糖转低血糖状态）和NHANES（吸烟史对比）上测试。最高未设限政策效用：BMI政策使用贝叶斯GMM为0.799，血糖政策使用硬性或加权FCM为0.735，吸烟史政策使用K-means为0.775。所有政策风险差异的95%置信区间都包含零，经Holm调整后无统计显著差异。贝叶斯池化通常保持未设限分配，而经验Bernstein设限更保守。相似效用的政策可能优先排序不同个体。

Conclusion: 研究结果应解释为假设依赖的决策支持证据，而非干预获益的证明。基于预处理特征的子组构建可为预算约束政策优先排序提供可行方法，但不同方法可能产生不同优先排序，需要谨慎解释。框架在观察性数据中提供了一种更稳定的政策评估方法。

Abstract: Conventional subgroup analyses can yield unstable and difficult-to-interpret conclusions, especially in observational biomedical data where each individual is observed under only one exposure state, true individual treatment effects are unavailable, and causal structure is uncertain. We investigate whether subgroups constructed from pretreatment characteristics, without using exposure, outcome, or estimated treatment-effect information, can serve as interpretable units for budget-constrained policy prioritization. We propose a framework combining causal-discovery-informed covariate selection, discovery-evaluation sample splitting, inductive unsupervised clustering, uncertainty-aware subgroup selection, and held-out doubly robust policy evaluation. We compare K-means, hard, membership-weighted, and stochastic Fuzzy C-means, Bayesian Gaussian mixture models, and a supervised causal-forest-derived CATE-tree comparator. Policies are evaluated under a 70% budget for hypothetical obesity-to-non-obesity and elevated-to-lower-glucose state shifts in the PIMA Indians Diabetes dataset and for a lifetime-smoking-history contrast in NHANES. The highest estimated ungated utilities were 0.799 for the BMI policy using Bayesian GMM, 0.735 for the glucose policy using hard or membership-weighted FCM, and 0.775 for the smoking-history policy using K-means. All paired 95% confidence intervals for policy-risk differences included zero, and no comparison remained statistically significant after Holm adjustment. Bayesian pooling generally preserved ungated allocations, whereas Empirical Bernstein gating was more conservative. Policies with similar estimated utility could nevertheless prioritize different individuals. The findings should be interpreted as assumption-dependent decision-support evidence for hypothetical state contrasts rather than proof of intervention benefit.

</details>


### [121] [The Art of Not Forgetting A Local Learning Architecture for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26523)
*Ashmith Atmuri,Yashaswini Rao Bhogarajula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CMP是一种持续学习架构，使用稀疏关系编码、两层竞争记忆和局部更新，无需端到端反向传播，在字节级语言建模中比Transformer+EWC表现出更低的灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究结合稀疏表示、局部学习和持久记忆是否能减少灾难性遗忘，相比传统的基于反向传播的持续学习方法。

Method: CMP架构将输入表示为稀疏关系编码，存储在两层竞争记忆中，通过局部更新学习，无需端到端反向传播。在域增量字节级语言建模协议上评估，与参数匹配的Transformer+EWC进行比较。

Result: CMP在15个域的实验中表现出稳定的遗忘行为，比Transformer基线遗忘更少。但存在单域准确率差距，在视觉基准上无效果，且无法与独立准确率提升机制结合。

Conclusion: 稀疏表示、局部学习和持久记忆的结合是持续学习的有前景方向，但需要进一步研究学习规则、表示和架构设计在缓解灾难性遗忘中的各自作用。

Abstract: We introduce CMP (Cognitive Memory Primitive), a continual-learning architecture that repre?sents inputs as sparse relational codes, stores them in a two-tier competitive memory, and learns through local updates without end-to-end backpropagation through its feature-generating system. We investigate whether combining sparse representations, local learning, and persistent memory can reduce catastrophic forgetting relative to conventional backpropagation-based continual?learning approaches. On a controlled domain-incremental byte-level language modeling protocol, CMP demonstrates substantially lower backward transfer than a parameter-matched Trans?former trained with online Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC). Across a three-seed replicated 15-domain experiment, CMP exhibits stable forgetting behavior, while separate head-to-head comparisons and domain-order analyses show consistently lower forgetting than the evaluated Transformer baseline under the reported experimental settings. We report these findings alongside a substantial single-domain accuracy gap relative to the Transformer, a null result on a vision benchmark, and a documented failure to combine CMP with an independent accuracy-improving mechanism, reflecting our commitment to reporting both positive and negative outcomes. These results suggest that the combination of sparse representations, local learning, and persistent memory is a promising direction for continual learning, while motivating further investigation into the respective roles of learning rules, representations, and architectural design in mitigating catastrophic forgetting.

</details>


### [122] [AgentGFM: A Graph Foundation Model with Node-Agent Information-Flow Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26533)
*Jingbo Cui,Jitao Zhao,Di Jin,Dongxiao He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AgentGFM：基于智能体的图神经网络，通过节点自主决策信息传播路径，实现自适应信息流控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图基础模型大多使用固定传播方案，无法适应不同节点间多样化的结构模式。需要让每个节点能自主决定信息如何在图中传播，即信息流控制能力。

Method: 将节点视为智能体，采用预测-行动-观察-纠正的交互过程。在行动阶段，节点做出三个决策：源接收、信号通道选择和增益感知的节点级停止。通过共享端到端可训练策略实现自适应信息流控制。

Result: 在节点级、图级和大规模迁移场景的广泛实验中，AgentGFM在不同图拓扑结构上表现出有效性。

Conclusion: AgentGFM通过智能体决策机制实现了自适应信息流控制，能够适应不同节点的结构变化，为图基础模型提供了更灵活的信息传播方案。

Abstract: Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) aim to learn transferable knowledge from multi-domain graphs and adapt to unseen scenarios. As a fundamental source of relational semantics in graphs, the transferability of topological patterns has long been central to GFM research. However, local structural patterns may vary across graphs and even among nodes within the same graph. Despite such structural variation, most existing GFMs rely on manually designed propagation schemes and apply them to new graphs largely unchanged. Such fixed schemes may not suit the diverse structural patterns of different nodes. This raises a key question: can each node autonomously determine how information should be propagated through the graph? We refer to this capability as information-flow control. Inspired by recent advances in agent technology, we formulate this problem as agent-based decision making and treat each node as an agent. Accordingly, we propose AgentGFM, in which all node agents follow a shared end-to-end trainable policy rather than using independent models. For adaptive information-flow control, each node interacts with the graph through a predict-act-observe-correct process. During the act stage, the node makes three decisions: source reception, signal-channel selection and gain-aware node-wise halting. The resulting observation is compared with the prediction and their discrepancy is used to correct the node state and guide subsequent interactions. Extensive experiments across node-level, graph-level and large-scale transfer scenarios demonstrate the effectiveness of AgentGFM across diverse graph topologies.

</details>


### [123] [From Tokens to Watt-hours: Analytical Energy Estimation for LLM Inference on Modern GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26571)
*Tina Vartziotis,Rodopi Kosteli,Elli Vartziotis,George Dasoulas,Michael Keckeisen,Konstantinos Skianis,Sotirios Kotsopoulos,Francesca Dominici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于分析建模和实证校准的GPU级LLM推理能耗估算方法，无需实际运行时测量即可在NVIDIA H100级加速器上估算能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理的运营能耗日益成为AI系统环境足迹的重要组成部分，但直接测量需要硬件遥测、功率仪器或基础设施特定监控，限制了在比较研究、早期系统设计和可持续性报告中的应用。

Method: 结合参数缩放Transformer FLOP计算、校准内存流量因子以及FP16/BF16张量核心计算和高速带宽内存移动的硬件特定能量系数。明确分离提示预填充和自回归解码，支持输入令牌、输出令牌和完整推理请求的能耗估算。将总能耗分解为计算、参数访问、键值缓存写入和注意力读取组件。

Result: 提供了一种透明、可重复且假设明确的近似方法，适用于模型比较、绿色编码分析和LLM推理工作负载的设计时评估，能够分析模型大小、上下文长度和生成令牌数量的扩展行为。

Conclusion: 该方法不是要取代物理功率测量，而是为LLM推理工作负载提供了一种适合模型比较、绿色编码分析和设计时评估的透明、可重复且假设明确的能耗估算方法。

Abstract: The operational energy consumption of large language model (LLM) inference is becoming an increasingly important component of the environmental footprint of deployed AI systems. However, direct measurement of inference energy often requires hardware telemetry, power instrumentation, or infrastructure-specific monitoring, limiting its applicability in comparative studies, early-stage system design, and sustainability reporting. This report presents an analytically structured, empirically calibrated, GPU-level methodology for estimating LLM inference energy on NVIDIA H100-class accelerators without direct runtime measurement. The proposed estimator combines parameter-scaled transformer FLOP accounting, calibrated memory-traffic factors, and hardware-specific energy coefficients for FP16/BF16 tensor-core computation and high-bandwidth-memory movement. It explicitly separates prompt prefill from autoregressive decoding, enabling energy estimates for input tokens, output tokens, and complete inference requests. The methodology further decomposes total energy into compute, parameter-access, key-value-cache write, and attention-read components, allowing the scaling behavior with model size, context length, and generated-token count to be analyzed. The resulting estimates are not intended to replace physical power measurements; rather, they provide transparent, reproducible, and assumption-explicit approximations suitable for model comparison, green-coding analysis, and design-time evaluation of LLM inference workloads.

</details>


### [124] [Simultaneous Coverage and Efficiency Guarantee in Online Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26577)
*Rahul Vaze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对现有自适应共形推理(ACI)方法的三个根本局限性，提出了一个统一的在线学习框架，同时控制绝对非抵消覆盖违规和预测集效率，并针对三种重要模型提供了动态演化的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应共形推理方法存在三个根本局限性：1）只控制符号长期覆盖误差，允许方向相反的补偿误差掩盖持续的错误覆盖；2）现有保证不涉及预测集大小，可能导致预测集过宽；3）效率保证基于固定预测器基准，在数据分布变化时基准变得不相关。

Method: 提出了统一的在线学习框架，针对三种模型：1）完全对抗性设置中，利用标准ACI更新是投影在线梯度下降的特性，推导同时覆盖和效率保证；2）随机设置中，提出滑动窗口分位数跟踪器并建立匹配的极小极大下界；3）协变量依赖随机设置中，开发分区ACI算法跟踪函数值oracle阈值。

Result: 在三种不同设置下都获得了同时控制覆盖违规和预测集效率的理论保证，包括在完全对抗性设置中无需分布或凸性假设的保证，随机设置中的速率最优算法，以及协变量依赖设置中的函数值oracle阈值跟踪算法。

Conclusion: 该研究克服了现有ACI方法的三个根本局限性，通过统一的在线学习框架，在不同设置下实现了同时控制绝对非抵消覆盖违规和预测集效率的目标，为在线共形预测提供了更全面和实用的理论保证。

Abstract: Adaptive conformal inference (ACI) of Gibbs and Cand{è}s and its variants are the standard approach to online conformal prediction under distribution shift, but they suffer from three fundamental limitations. First, their guarantees control only the \emph{signed} long-run coverage error: persistent miscoverage in one direction can be masked by compensating errors later, so a method can satisfy the theoretical guarantee while being badly wrong for extended periods. Second, existing guarantees say nothing about prediction-set size, so validity can be achieved trivially at the cost of unduly wide prediction sets. Third, the efficiency guarantees that do exist compare against a \emph{fixed} predictor chosen in hindsight, a benchmark that becomes increasingly less meaningful once the data-generating distribution shifts, since the very notion of an optimal threshold then changes over time.
  We consider a unified online learning framework that simultaneously controls absolute, non-cancelling coverage violation and prediction-set efficiency against a dynamically evolving benchmark for three important models. In the fully adversarial setting, exploiting the fact that the standard ACI update is exactly projected online gradient descent on the pinball loss, we derive simultaneous coverage and efficiency guarantees for arbitrary monotone Lipschitz efficiency objectives, with no distributional or {\it convexity} assumptions. In the stochastic setting with full-score feedback, we propose a sliding-window quantile tracker and establish a matching minimax lower bound showing our algorithm is rate-optimal. In the covariate-dependent stochastic setting, we develop a partitioned ACI algorithm that tracks a function-valued oracle threshold, and derive simultaneous coverage and efficiency guarantees.

</details>


### [125] [Benchmarking ConvLSTM for One-Day-Ahead IMDAA Rainfall-Field Prediction across Four Indian Cities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26581)
*Tanmay Ghosh,Shaurabh Anand,Rakesh Gomaji Nannewar,Nithin Nagaraj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究测试了ConvLSTM在印度四个城市日尺度降雨预测中的表现，发现ConvLSTM并未持续优于更简单的模型，架构选择应基于全面的基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ConvLSTM广泛用于降水预报，但其性能证据主要来自密集的高频雷达序列。本研究旨在测试卷积循环网络在小型日尺度再分析网格上的单日降雨场预测中是否具有优势。

Method: 使用印度季风数据同化与分析(IMDAA)1998-2020年6-9月数据，分析班加罗尔、德里、加尔各答和孟买四个城市。比较了10种朴素方法、统计方法、基于树的方法和神经网络方法，使用纯大气输入和降雨历史+大气输入两种输入方式。评估了完整场、区域平均降雨、空间异常和高降雨日的性能。

Result: ConvLSTM并未持续优于更简单的替代方法。FC-LSTM在班加罗尔、加尔各答和孟买的区域平均降雨误差最低，而持续性在德里表现最佳。ConvLSTM仅在孟买的空间异常误差最低。神经网络模型低估了降雨量并预测了过少的阈值超限，而持续性在每个城市都获得了最高的检测性能。模型对最新输入日最敏感，孟买显示出更广泛的近期滞后敏感性。

Conclusion: 网格化输入本身并不能证明ConvLSTM的优势，架构选择应基于对平均性能、空间性能和高降雨性能的全面基准测试。ConvLSTM和FC-LSTM之间的差异很小，需要谨慎选择模型架构。

Abstract: Convolutional long short-term memory networks (ConvLSTMs) are widely used for precipitation forecasting, but most evidence for their performance comes from dense, high-frequency radar sequences. This study tests whether convolutional recurrence improves one-day-ahead rainfall-field prediction on small daily reanalysis grids. Indian Monsoon Data Assimilation and Analysis (IMDAA) fields for June-September 1998-2020 were analysed for Bengaluru, Delhi, Kolkata and Mumbai. Ten naive, statistical, tree-based and neural approaches were compared using atmospheric-only and rainfall-history-plus-atmospheric inputs. Performance was assessed for complete fields, domain-mean rainfall, spatial anomalies and high-rainfall days.
  ConvLSTM did not consistently outperform simpler alternatives. FC-LSTM produced the numerically lowest domain-mean rainfall error in Bengaluru, Kolkata and Mumbai, whereas persistence performed best in Delhi. ConvLSTM produced the numerically lowest spatial-anomaly error only in Mumbai, where rainfall fields showed greater short-term spatial continuity and rainfall-history inputs improved all three neural architectures. The difference between ConvLSTM and FC-LSTM was nevertheless small. Neural models underestimated rainfall magnitude and predicted too few threshold exceedances on high-rainfall days, while persistence achieved the highest detection performance in every city. Post-hoc analyses showed that the selected models were most sensitive to the latest input day, with broader recent-lag sensitivity in Mumbai. These findings show that gridded inputs alone do not justify ConvLSTM and that architecture choice should follow strong benchmarking across average, spatial and high-rainfall performance.

</details>


### [126] [Uncertainty-Guided LLM Semantic Augmentation for Heterogeneous Treatment Effect Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26599)
*Jialu Xu,Mengkun Liang,Guannan Liu,Xiaojie Mao,Junjie Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CURL：一种基于因果不确定性引导的表示学习方法，通过分离的分配通道和异质性通道来提升CATE估计的稳定性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 估计异质性处理效应对于精准干预至关重要，但CATE估计面临局部不稳定问题，原始数值和分类编码可能使语义关系和高阶交互隐式化，导致联合任务不稳定

Method: 提出CURL方法，使用估计器不确定性来分配预训练语义能力给局部不稳定单元，通过两个角色条件提示查询冻结的LLM，从观察协变量构建分配导向和异质性导向的表示，并通过分离的路径路由

Result: 在四个基准测试中，CURL在大多数设置下改进了十个宿主学习器，消融研究、细化动态、路由重新分配和探测分析支持了设计意图和两个通道的作用

Conclusion: CURL通过分离的分配和异质性通道有效解决了CATE估计的局部不稳定问题，利用预训练LLM的语义能力提升异质性处理效应估计的准确性

Abstract: Estimating heterogeneous treatment effects is central to targeted interventions, such as personalized promotions and precision medicine. We focus on the conditional average treatment effect (CATE), a standard estimand for characterizing such heterogeneity. Even under standard identification conditions, finite-sample CATE estimation requires learning the nuisance structure for covariate adjustment and treatment-effect heterogeneity, often together with an effective representation of X. Raw numerical and categorical encodings can leave semantic relations and higher-order interactions implicit, making this joint task locally unstable. A motivating study further shows that this instability appears through partially separable assignment- and heterogeneity-side channels. Building on this observation, we propose CURL (Causal Uncertainty-guided Representation Learning), a plug-in adapter that uses estimator uncertainty to allocate pretrained semantic capacity to locally unstable units. CURL queries a frozen LLM through two role-conditioned prompts, constructs assignment- and heterogeneity-oriented representations from the observed covariates, and routes them through separated pathways. On four benchmarks, CURL improves ten host learners in most settings, while ablation, refinement-dynamics, route-reassignment, and probe analyses support the intended design and roles of the two channels.

</details>


### [127] [FedWeave: Rethinking the Unit of Specialization in Heterogeneous Federated MoE-LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26618)
*Donghang Duan,Xu Zheng,Lizong Zhang,Chong Mu,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedWeave是一个联邦学习框架，通过非对称聚合解决多任务异构性下的专家污染问题，使用原型级专家聚合和混合任务路由器训练，在稀疏推理时保持接近软路由的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦PEFT方法（如Federated MoE-LoRA）假设客户端任务一致，但实际上客户端内部可能存在多个任务，导致专家更新污染和梯度冲突，需要更精细的聚合策略。

Method: 采用非对称聚合：专家通过无监督原型发现形成局部桶并在客户端间对齐，进行原型级聚合；路由器使用混合任务轨迹训练。推理时进行稀疏推理（仅激活一个专家）。

Result: 在异构多任务基准测试中，FedWeave持续优于现有基线方法，消融实验验证了其设计的有效性，稀疏推理几乎保持全部软路由性能。

Conclusion: FedWeave通过分离专家聚合和路由器优化，解决了联邦学习中任务异构性导致的专家污染问题，理论分析解释了非对称聚合的优势，并在实际应用中表现出色。

Abstract: Federated PEFT enables LLMs to collaboratively adapt to decentralized private data without sharing raw examples. However, task heterogeneity across clients can cause cross-task interference and gradient conflicts during aggregation. Federated MoE-LoRA addresses this challenge through specialized LoRA experts and conditional routing. Yet existing methods typically specialize at client granularity, implicitly assuming task-coherent clients. Our core insight is that experts need purity, namely pattern-coherent updates that preserve specialization, whereas routers need contrast, namely mixed-task observations that support expert comparison. We propose FedWeave, a framework that adopts asymmetric aggregation, separating expert aggregation from router optimization to meet these two requirements. FedWeave uses unsupervised prototype discovery to form local buckets and align them across clients, enabling prototype-level expert aggregation while retaining mixed-task client trajectories for router training. At inference, FedWeave performs sparse inference with one active expert while preserving nearly all soft-routing performance. Our theoretical analysis explains why asymmetric aggregation is advantageous: it controls expert convergence in stationarity through off-pattern contamination, identifies the consensus error induced by fragmented router trajectories, and bounds sparse-inference risk. On a heterogeneous multi-task benchmark with mainstream LLM backbones, FedWeave consistently outperforms strong baselines, while ablations verify the effectiveness of our design.

</details>


### [128] [Enhancing Automated Machine Learning via Homogeneous Train-Test Splitting Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26625)
*Yearn Tan Yin Tze,Charles Grellois*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了训练-测试分割中的统计相似性问题，提出了一种将相似性作为明确优化目标的优化分布方法，在15个UCI基准数据集上取得了最高的平均MMD相似度(89.0%)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习中的准确模型评估严重依赖于数据集如何分割为训练和测试子集。标准随机分割假设两个分区共享相同的底层分布，这一假设在存在类别不平衡、自然聚类或空间自相关的数据集中经常被违反。本文研究了统计相似性在训练-测试分割中的作用及其对AutoML模型评估的影响。

Method: 比较了五种已建立的策略：随机分割、分层抽样、Kennard-Stone、Duplex和SPXY。使用卡方检验、Kolmogorov-Smirnov检验和最大均值差异(MMD)测试评估相似性。提出了优化分布方法，将相似性作为明确的优化目标。

Result: 基于几何的方法产生接近零的MMD分数，给下游性能估计带来了不稳定性。提出的优化分布方法在所有评估策略中实现了最高的平均MMD相似度，达到89.0%。

Conclusion: 训练-测试分割中的统计相似性对模型评估有重要影响，优化分布方法通过将相似性作为明确优化目标，能够产生更稳定和可靠的性能估计。

Abstract: Accurate model evaluation in machine learning depends critically on how datasets are split into training and testing subsets. Standard random splitting assumes that both partitions share the same underlying distribution, an assumption often violated in datasets with class imbalance, natural clustering, or spatial autocorrelation. This paper investigates the role of statistical similarity in train-test splitting and its consequences for AutoML model evaluation. Five established strategies are compared across fifteen UCI benchmark datasets: random splitting, stratified sampling, Kennard-Stone, Duplex, and SPXY. Similarity is assessed using chi-square, Kolmogorov-Smirnov, and Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) tests. Geometry-based methods consistently produce near-zero MMD scores, introducing instability into downstream performance estimates. The proposed Optimised-Distribution method treats similarity as an explicit optimisation objective and achieves the highest mean MMD similarity, 89.0%, across all strategies evaluated.

</details>


### [129] [Understanding Context Sampling in TabPFN on Small Tabular Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26628)
*Mohammed Abdullah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究TabPFN在小型表格数据集上使用不同上下文选择策略对预测稳定性、准确性和选择成本的影响，发现随机采样因其提供特征空间覆盖而有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TabPFN通过上下文学习进行分类，但实践中需要选择上下文大小和哪些行构成上下文。研究者希望了解这些选择如何影响预测稳定性、准确性和选择成本。

Method: 在15个OpenML数据集上使用重复上下文采样进行研究，分析不同上下文大小、采样方法（均匀随机采样、K-Means、最远点采样）对预测性能的影响。

Result: 更大的上下文既更准确也更稳定；多样性（beta=+0.23）和覆盖范围是准确性的更强预测因子；K-Means和最远点采样与随机采样准确性相似但选择成本高2-3个数量级。

Conclusion: 随机采样之所以有效是因为它在期望上提供了特征空间覆盖，而不是因为复制了底层数据分布。实践中应优先使用随机采样而非昂贵的采样方法。

Abstract: TabPFN performs classification through in-context learning: it conditions on a set of labeled training rows (the context, or prototypes) and predicts test labels without gradient updates. On small tabular datasets, practitioners must still choose the context size and which rows constitute the context. We study how these choices affect prediction stability, accuracy, and selection cost using repeated context sampling on 15 OpenML datasets. Specifically, we investigate (i) whether larger contexts reduce prediction variability across random draws, (ii) whether accuracy depends on preserving the training distribution or on feature-space coverage, and (iii) whether expensive selection methods such as K-Means and farthest-point sampling provide benefits over uniform random sampling.
  We find that larger contexts are both more accurate and substantially more stable, with AUC coefficient of variation decreasing from roughly 6 to 18% at k=16 to 1 to 4% at larger context sizes on datasets with room for improvement. Although accuracy correlates with distribution representativeness in random contexts, controlled experiments show that matching feature means alone can reduce accuracy by up to 0.5 AUC because it reduces context diversity. Mixed-effects analysis identifies diversity and coverage, rather than feature-mean matching, as the stronger predictor of accuracy (diversity beta=+0.23, p=3x10^-12; feature-mean shift beta=-0.01, p=0.71). K-Means and farthest-point sampling achieve similar accuracy to random selection while requiring two to three orders of magnitude more selection cost. These results show that random sampling succeeds because it provides feature-space coverage in expectation, not because it reproduces the underlying data distribution.

</details>


### [130] [RAG-HAR+: Towards Cost-Efficient LLM-Based Human Activity Recognition for Edge Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26631)
*Hansi Karunarathna,Nirhoshan Sivaroopan,Chamara Madarasingha,Anura Jayasumana,Kanchana Thilakarathna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAG-HAR+是一种基于检索增强生成的人体活动识别方法，通过检索优先、成本优化的设计，减少对大型语言模型的依赖，同时保持或提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法需要针对特定数据集训练、大量标注数据，并且需要不断适应新的传感器设置或活动分类。这限制了人体活动识别在医疗保健、康复、健身追踪等领域的应用灵活性。

Method: RAG-HAR+采用检索优先的方法：1）使用离线检索设计代理从多样化运动描述符池中设计数据集特定的特征组；2）推理时，对具有强检索证据的样本使用检索邻居的多数投票；3）仅将不确定案例交给基于LLM的歧义解析代理处理。

Result: 在六个HAR基准测试中，RAG-HAR+在保持竞争性或改进性能的同时，显著减少了LLM使用、令牌消耗和推理时间。移动原型展示了检索优先、LLM辅助的HAR在移动传感场景中的实际可行性。

Conclusion: RAG-HAR+提供了一种训练免费、检索增强的人体活动识别方法，通过优化检索和减少对LLM的依赖，实现了高效、实用的活动识别解决方案。

Abstract: Human Activity Recognition (HAR) from wearable sensors supports applications in healthcare, rehabilitation, fitness tracking, and smart environments. Yet, existing deep learning approaches require dataset-specific training, large labeled corpora, and repeated adaptation to new sensor settings or activity taxonomies. Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Human Activity Recognition (RAG-HAR) addresses this by framing HAR as a training-free, retrieval-augmented task, in which statistical descriptions of sensor windows are used to retrieve similar labeled examples that guide LLM-based classification. We introduce RAG-HAR+, a retrieval-first and cost-optimized extension that strengthens retrieval while reducing dependence on LLM-based inference. RAG-HAR+ uses an offline Retrieval Designer Agent to design dataset-specific feature groups from a diverse pool of motion descriptors, enabling sensor windows to be compared using features better aligned with dataset-specific activity patterns. During inference, RAG-HAR+ uses majority voting over retrieved neighbors for samples with strong retrieval evidence and defers only uncertain cases to an LLM-based Ambiguity Resolver Agent. Across six HAR benchmarks, RAG-HAR+ maintains competitive or improved performance while reducing LLM usage, token consumption, and inference time. We further extend the RAG-HAR mobile prototype to demonstrate the practical feasibility of retrieval-first, LLM-assisted HAR in mobile sensing scenarios.

</details>


### [131] [AIGen: Automating AI Bill of Materials Generation Through Hybrid MLOps Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26652)
*Federica Pepe,Daniele Bifolco,Costantino Martignetti,Aureliano D'Amici,Fabiano Izzo,Damian A. Tamburri,Massimiliano Di Penta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AIGen是一个基于MLflow的模块化AI物料清单生成工具，通过结合挖掘启发式方法和大型语言模型，自动生成符合SPDX 3.0 AI标准的AI系统组件清单，支持多种AI框架并便于扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统需要对其构成组件（如数据集、模型权重、训练流水线等）进行严格文档化以确保负责任的发展。虽然SPDX 3.0标准引入了对AI和数据集配置的原生支持，但能够自动化生成标准兼容的AI物料清单的实用工具仍然稀缺。

Method: AIGen基于MLflow MLOps框架构建，结合挖掘启发式方法和大型语言模型生成AI物料清单。通过插件接口支持扩展，无需修改核心代码即可添加特定领域收集器，支持Hugging Face、PyTorch、TensorFlow等多种异构AI框架。

Result: AIGen能够生成机器可读、可互操作的AI系统组件清单，完全符合SPDX 3.0 AI配置标准。该工具提供了具体、可重用的基础，支持AI供应链治理的透明度和问责制。

Conclusion: AIGen为AI系统的负责任开发提供了实用工具支持，有助于遵守欧盟AI法案、NIST AI风险管理框架和ISO/IEC 42001等法规标准，促进透明、负责任的AI供应链治理。

Abstract: The responsible development and deployment of artificial intelligence (AI) systems requires rigorous documentation of their constituent artifacts, e.g., datasets, model weights, training pipelines, and runtime dependencies. Although the Software Package Data Exchange (SPDX) 3.0 standard introduced native support for AI and dataset profiles, practical tooling capable of generating standards-compliant AI Bills of Materials (AIBoMs) in an automated and extensible manner remains scarce. This paper presents AIGen, a modular AIBoM generator that produces machine-readable, interoperable inventories of AI system components that comply with the SPDX 3.0 AI profile. AIGen works on top of the MLflow MLOps framework and combines mining heuristics with Large Language Models to generate AIBoMs. A plugin interface allows practitioners to extend the tool with domain-specific collectors without modifying the core codebase, supporting heterogeneous AI frameworks such as Hugging Face, PyTorch, and TensorFlow. AIGen is designed to facilitate compliance with the European Union AI Act, the NIST AI Risk Management Framework, and ISO/IEC 42001, providing a concrete, reusable foundation for transparent, accountable AI supply chain governance. Tool URL: https://github.com/danielebifolco/AIGen Tool Video: https://youtu.be/\_nAbXDWfVL4

</details>


### [132] [Efficient Heteroscedastic Bayesian Optimization for Risk-Aware AutoRL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26680)
*Mingxuan Che,Tsung-Yuan Tseng,Theresa Eimer,Marius Lindauer,Alexander von Rohr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ERAHBO是一种高效的异方差贝叶斯优化方法，用于强化学习超参数调优，同时优化平均回报和降低训练运行间的变异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习的结果具有高度随机性，其期望性能和变异性都依赖于超参数配置。需要一种方法在优化平均回报的同时降低变异性，并提高超参数优化的样本效率。

Method: 提出ERAHBO（高效风险规避异方差贝叶斯优化）方法，将学习结果的均值和方差都建模为超参数配置的函数。采用自适应重采样而非固定预算策略来提高样本效率。

Result: 在多种RL算法和环境中的实证评估表明，ERAHBO通常优于风险中性和风险规避的基线方法，在风险规避回报方面提供了改进的样本效率。

Conclusion: ERAHBO是一种有效的超参数优化方法，能够在保证高平均回报的同时降低结果变异性，并通过自适应采样提高优化效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has shown remarkable success across a wide range of complex tasks. However, RL outcomes can be highly stochastic, and both expected performance and variability often depend on hyperparameter (HP) configurations. We propose efficient and risk-averse heteroscedastic Bayesian Optimization (ERAHBO), a Bayesian optimization method that models both the mean and variance of learning outcomes as functions of the HP configurations. ERAHBO aims to identify HP configurations that achieve high average return while reducing variability across training runs, and it improves the sample efficiency of the HP optimization via adaptive re-sampling rather than a fixed budget per HP. Empirical evaluations across diverse RL algorithms and environments demonstrate that ERAHBO generally outperforms both risk-neutral and risk-averse baselines, delivering improved sample efficiency for risk-averse returns.

</details>


### [133] [Universality and Approximation Rates of Graph Neural Networks with Random Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26699)
*Lukas Gonon,Thilo Meyer-Brandis,Niklas Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了带有随机节点特征的消息传递图神经网络，证明了这类神经网络在近似任意可测的置换不变或置换等变函数方面的普遍性能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机节点特征已被证明在理论和实践上都能增强图神经网络的表达能力。本文旨在建立一种新的普遍性结果，证明带有部分随机节点特征的置换等变神经网络能够近似任意可测的置换不变或置换等变函数。

Method: 使用置换等变神经网络（PENNs），这是一种从前馈神经网络组件构建的GNN类别，包含了许多重要的GNN架构。研究结合了部分随机节点特征，并分析了PENNs在固定大小有向图上的近似能力。

Result: 证明了PENNs结合部分随机节点特征能够以任意精度概率近似任意可测的置换不变或置换等变函数。对于k次连续可微函数（k≥2），还推导了近似率的上界，将PENN前馈组件的复杂性（层深度和非零权重数量）与所需近似精度联系起来。

Conclusion: 该研究为带有随机节点特征的图神经网络提供了坚实的理论保证，证明了它们在近似置换不变和等变函数方面的普遍性能力，并为实际应用中的网络复杂度选择提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We investigate message-passing graph neural networks with random node features. Random node features are known to enhance the expressiveness of graph neural networks (GNNs) both theoretically and empirically. Here, we establish a novel universality result focusing on permutation-equivariant neural networks (PENNs), a class of GNNs built from feedforward neural network components that subsumes many prominent GNN architectures. We show that PENNs, combined with partially random node features, can approximate arbitrarily well in probability any measurable permutation-invariant or permutation-equivariant function on directed graphs of fixed size with multidimensional node and edge features. For $k$-times continuously differentiable functions, $k\geq 2$, we also derive upper bounds on the approximation rates, relating the complexity of the feedforward components of a PENN in terms of layer depth and number of nonzero weights to the desired approximation accuracy.

</details>


### [134] [Mixture-of-experts for handwriting trajectory reconstruction from IMU sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26708)
*Florent Imbert,Eric Anquetil,Yann Soullard,Romain Tavenard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于混合专家(MOE)的方法，用于数字笔的在线手写轨迹重建，通过分别处理触摸和悬停轨迹来提升重建精度，并发布了新的公开基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字笔作为人机交互的常用工具，能够在任何表面书写并保留数字轨迹，可用于课堂书写辅助学习。现有方法在精细重建触摸轨迹和准确分析悬停部分以正确定位下一个触摸轨迹方面存在不足。

Method: 提出混合专家(MOE)方法：1)触摸专家模型专门处理铅笔触摸轨迹；2)悬停专家模型专门处理悬停笔轨迹。通过额外上下文或特定示例改进每个专家的学习。

Result: 实验结果相比主要竞争对手显示出显著提升。同时发布了新的公开基准数据集，为未来手写重建研究提供比较基准。

Conclusion: 该方法能更精细地重建触摸轨迹，同时精确分析悬停部分以正确定位下一个触摸痕迹，为数字笔手写轨迹重建提供了有效解决方案，有助于课堂书写辅助学习应用。

Abstract: The use of digital pens for online handwriting trajectory reconstruction is a prevalent method for human-computer interaction. In this study, we focus on a digital pen equipped with sensors where we aim at reconstructing the online handwriting trajectory. This pen enables writing on any surface and preserving the digital trace of handwriting. This type of pen could be used as an aid to learning to write in classroom. In this paper, we propose a new approach learning to finely reconstruct the touching trajectories while precisely analyzing the hovering part in order to position the next touching trace correctly. This relies on a Mixture-Of-Experts (MOE) approach. The first expert is dedicated for the pencil touch, and is named touching expert model. The second one is dedicated for the hovering pen trajectory, and is named hovering expert model. We improve on the learning of each of these experts based on additional context or specific examples. In addition we introduce a novel public benchmark dataset, to enable future research and comparisons in the field of handwriting reconstruction. The results demonstrates a significant enhancement compared to its primary competitors.

</details>


### [135] [PowerAtlas: Towards Electricity-Computing Co-Scheduling for Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26710)
*Kaiwen Jiang,Siya Xu,Ziyue Zhu,Chao Yang,Anh Tuan Luu,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PowerAtlas是一个LLM智能体框架，用于电力-计算协同调度，通过整合历史实例、领域知识和物理约束，生成满足电网运行规则和计算任务SLA的联合决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI工作负载快速增长，数据中心成为大规模、波动但时空灵活的电网负载，急需协调电力-计算调度。通用大语言模型生成的调度方案在严格电网约束下常常不可行，导致线路过载和未服务负载。

Method: PowerAtlas是一个LLM智能体框架，整合历史实例、领域知识和物理约束，生成满足电网运行规则和计算任务服务级别协议（SLA）的联合决策。在中国某省级电力公司合作下，建立了实验性电力-计算网络，并在真实数据中心数据上验证决策循环。

Result: 从去标识化运营数据构建了ECBench基准测试，包含2000个调度实例和最优解。在11个LLM上的实验表明，PowerAtlas在现实物理运行条件下有效，在三个开源权重骨干模型上保持一致的可行性和成本收益。

Conclusion: PowerAtlas框架能够有效解决AI工作负载快速增长带来的电力-计算协同调度问题，在严格电网约束下生成可行且优化的调度方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: The rapid growth of AI workloads is turning data centers into large-scale, volatile, yet spatiotemporally flexible grid loads, creating an urgent need for coordinated electricity-computing scheduling. Under stringent grid constraints, schedules from general-purpose large language models (LLMs) are often infeasible, causing line-flow violations and unserved load. We present PowerAtlas, an LLM-agent framework for electricity-computing co-scheduling that integrates historical instances, domain knowledge, and physical constraints to produce joint decisions satisfying both grid operational rules and the service-level agreements (SLAs) of computing tasks. Working with a provincial power utility in China, we built an experimental electricity-computing network and validated the decision loop on real data-center data; from de-identified operational data we further constructed ECBench, a benchmark of 2,000 scheduling instances with oracle-optimal solutions. Experiments across eleven LLMs demonstrate the effectiveness of PowerAtlas under realistic physical operating conditions, with consistent feasibility and cost gains across three open-weight backbones. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/JAVA-Jiang/PowerAtlas.

</details>


### [136] [Domain adaptation for handwriting trajectory reconstruction from IMU sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26736)
*Florent Imbert,Romain Tavenard,Yann Soullard,Eric Anquetil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究针对配备运动传感器的数字笔，探讨了成人与儿童书写信号差异问题，提出了一种域适应方法来构建统一的中间特征表示，以促进轨迹重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字笔作为教育工具在课堂上辅助书写学习具有重要价值，但成人与儿童书写时传感器信号存在显著差异（由于速度、书写信心不同），这影响了数字轨迹的准确获取。

Method: 采用域适应方法构建统一的中间特征表示来处理成人与儿童书写信号的差异，并与从头训练模型和微调模型两种方法进行比较。

Result: 研究表明域适应方法在利用现有知识应用于不同情境方面具有优势，能够有效处理成人与儿童书写信号的差异问题。

Conclusion: 域适应方法为解决数字笔在成人和儿童书写信号差异问题提供了有效解决方案，展示了该技术在教育应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Digital pens are commonly used to write on digital devices, providing the handwriting trace and enhancing human-computer interation. This study focuses on a digital pen equipped with kinematic sensors, allowing users to write on any surface while simultaneously preserving a digital trajectory of handwriting. This technology holds significant potential as a valuable educational tool, particularly in classrooms where it can facilitate the process of learning to write. A major issue is based on the difference in captured signals between adults and children. For similar handwriting trace, we have large differences in sensor signals due to differences in speed and confidence in the handwriting gesture of children. To address this, we investigate a domain adaptation approach to build a unified intermediate feature representation aimed at facilitating the trajectory reconstruction. We demonstrate the interest of domain adaptation methods in leveraging existing knowledge for application in different contexts. Specifically, we compare our domain adaptation approach with two other methods: training the model from scratch and fine-tuning the model.

</details>


### [137] [CalTwin: Towards Calibrated, Shift-Robust Medical World Models via Fisher-Information Regularisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26752)
*Behraj Khan,Shabir Ahmad,Syed Ahmad Chan Bukhari,Tahir Qasim Syed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CalTwin方法，通过单一轻量级正则化目标解决医学世界模型中的协变量偏移和置信度错位问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学世界模型在临床部署中面临两个主要可靠性威胁：1) 协变量偏移 - 训练数据分散在不同医院、扫描仪和时间段，导致特征分布不一致；2) 置信度错位 - 多步预测在临床风险最高区域往往过于自信

Method: 提出CalTwin方法，结合基于Fisher信息的偏移惩罚（来自先前协变量偏移研究）和置信度错位惩罚（来自先前校准视觉语言分类研究），应用于基于GRU的医学世界模型的潜在状态转移预测器

Result: 在PhysioNet 2019 Sepsis Challenge上评估：CalTwin将OOD下一步潜在状态MSE降低9.1%（FIM惩罚单独贡献7.0%）；置信度错位惩罚带来的ECE降低确实存在但较小（CalTwin降低0.7%，CMP单独降低1.3%）

Conclusion: 协变量偏移和置信度错位问题可以通过统一的轻量级正则化目标CalTwin有效解决，该方法在医学世界模型中显著改善了分布外泛化能力

Abstract: Medical world models aim to learn a latent state of patient or organ physiology and a transition function that forecasts how that state evolves under interventions, supporting downstream tasks from imaging-based diagnosis to digital-twin treatment planning. Two failure modes threaten the reliability of such models in clinical deployment: (i)~\emph{covariate shift}, because training data are fragmented across hospitals, scanners, and time, so the feature distribution seen by the latent-dynamics predictor differs across fragments and from the distribution at deployment; and (ii)~\emph{confidence misalignment}, because multi-step forecasts are often overconfident exactly where clinical risk is highest. We argue that both problems admit a unified treatment via a single lightweight regularisation objective, \textbf{CalTwin}, which combines a Fisher-Information-based shift penalty adapted from our prior work on fragmented covariate-shift remediation~\cite{khan2025mitigating,khan2025causal} with a Confidence Misalignment Penalty adapted from our prior work on calibrated vision-language classification~\cite{khan2025confidence}, applied here to a GRU-based medical world model's latent transition predictor. We derive the combined objective, establish which proof steps transfer from the classification setting without modification and which require adaptation, and evaluate it on the PhysioNet 2019 Sepsis Challenge, treating the two hospital systems as sequential training fragments and the unseen system as an out-of-distribution test. CalTwin reduces OOD next-step latent-state MSE by 9.1\% relative to the no-penalty baseline (FIM penalty alone accounts for 7.0\%); the ECE reduction from the Confidence Misalignment Penalty is real but small (0.7\% for CalTwin, 1.3\% for CMP alone).

</details>


### [138] [Journey Operators for Structured Multi-Axis Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26775)
*Mahesh Godavarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于建模多轴结构的理论框架，其中数据项携带内容及每个轴的变换，路径操作由沿路径的轴变换乘积定义，解释了RoPE类方法为何自然出现，并基于此设计了JoFormer模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多数据（如句子、图像、音频等）具有多轴结构，其中某些轴顺序重要，而独立轴之间的组合或移动不应依赖轴顺序。现有方法缺乏对这种多轴结构的系统建模框架。

Method: 开发了一个理论框架，每个数据项携带内容和各轴的变换。路径连接两个位置定义"旅程"，旅程算子是沿路径的每轴变换乘积，控制数据组合和相对位置描述。当变换固定时恢复RoPE及其多维变体；当变换依赖数据时获得内容自适应位置归纳偏置。

Result: 证明了路径良定义的条件：当轴变换可交换时，跨轴的组合和移动是路径无关的。在给定的对称性、余循环、双线性和范数保持假设下，证明了成对评分规则必须采用分块旋转形式，解释了RoPE类方法的自然出现。

Conclusion: 基于该理论设计了JoFormer模型用于值聚合，并将其与注意力和状态空间模型联系起来。在视觉、语言和长度泛化上的初步实验表明，这些归纳偏置在实践中具有可观察的影响。

Abstract: Many kinds of data have structure along one or more axes: words in a sentence, pixels in an image, nodes in a tree, frames in audio, or cells in a 3D volume. Along one axis, order matters: "the dog bit the man" is different from "the man bit the dog." Across independent axes, however, neither composition nor movement should depend on the order of axes: in an image, composing right then down should give the same result as composing down then right, and moving right then down should describe the same relative position as moving down then right.
  We develop a framework for modeling this kind of multi-axis structure. Each data item carries its content together with a small transformation for each axis. A path connecting two positions defines a journey; the journey operator is the product of per-axis transformations along that path, governing both how data composes along the path and how relative position is described. When the transformations are fixed, our framework recovers Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) and its multi-dimensional variants. When they depend on the data, the model gains a content-adaptive positional inductive bias.
  We show exactly when these paths are well-defined: both composition and movement across axes are path-independent precisely when the axis transformations commute. We also prove that, under the stated toral-frame symmetry, cocycle, bilinearity, and norm-preservation assumptions, the resulting pairwise scoring rule must take the form of block-wise rotations, explaining why RoPE-like methods arise naturally.
  Finally, we use this theory to design JoFormer, a model for value aggregation, and relate it to attention and state-space models (SSMs).
  Initial experiments across vision, language, and length generalization suggest that these inductive biases can have observable consequences in
  practice.

</details>


### [139] [SkillRise: Agentic Reinforcement Learning for Cross-Task Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26784)
*Zhiyuan Yao,Yuxin Chen,Zhengxi Lu,Zishan Xu,Yueqing Sun,Yifu Guo,Yuquan Lu,Zhengzhou Cai,Kangning Zhang,Zhuowen Han,Zi-Han Wang,Ziang Ye,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,Weiwen Liu,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SkillRise是一个统一的强化学习框架，通过跨任务学习可重用技能，将相关任务实例组织成渐进式序列，使用单一策略交替执行任务解决和技能文档更新，在多个基准测试中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将任务视为独立事件，或专注于单一任务的重复尝试，缺乏跨任务学习可重用技能的统一框架。SkillRise旨在解决这一问题，让LLM智能体能够提取、精炼和重用跨任务的可转移技能。

Method: SkillRise将相关任务实例组织成渐进式挑战序列，使用单一策略在任务解决和技能文档更新之间交替。技能文档直接传递给下一个任务，通过跨任务解耦信用分配来监督当前任务结果和折扣下游结果。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld上的实验显示，SkillRise在Pass@1性能上优于所有对比方法，比最强基线提升2.3-8.5个百分点。即使在不同任务上训练，其学习到的技能更新策略对同一任务的重复尝试仍然有效。

Conclusion: SkillRise提供了一个简单高效的训练范式，使LLM智能体能够跨任务提取、精炼和重用可转移技能，显著减少多阶段技能学习管道的运行时开销，并在测试时展现出跨任务的扩展性。

Abstract: Large language model agents often encounter related yet distinct tasks that share reusable solution patterns. Yet standard agentic reinforcement learning treats tasks as independent episodes, while existing approaches to skill learning either focus on repeated attempts of one task or use pipelines with multiple stages that entangle extraction, retrieval, and execution. We introduce SkillRise, a unified reinforcement learning framework for learning skills across tasks. SkillRise organizes related instances into progressively challenging sequences and uses a single policy to alternate between task solving and curating an evolving skill document passed directly to the next task. Decoupled credit assignment across tasks supervises solving with the current task outcome and curation with discounted downstream outcomes. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld show that SkillRise achieves the strongest Pass@1 performance among the compared methods, with gains over the strongest baseline ranging from 2.3 to 8.5 percentage points. Although trained across distinct tasks, its learned curation policy remains effective for repeated attempts on the same task.
